Nonlinear correlated geometry remains required.\n- **DANGEROUS:** \"The last-sample deletion can be repeated backwards.\" Fact1252b8823bb5a290 retains all queried-prefix constraints.\n- **DANGEROUS:** \"The four local silver witnesses globalize.\" Draft66067b85d1228261 passes all four at silver capacity but is defeated by an endpoint cut.\n- **DANGEROUS:** \"Failure of normal mixtures means all polynomial methods fail.\" Nonnormal correlations, higher-order clocks and more general structured kernels are outside that exclusion.\n- **DANGEROUS:** \"A fixed-prefix last-step minimax exchange optimizes every step.\" The fixed-prefix mixture result and the joint nonconvexity counterexample do not authorize earlier exchanges.\n\nDo not discard old planes, old query heights or the true minimizer cost. Do not infer unrestricted upper bounds from failed lower-witness families. Do not promote a complete-looking draft to a fact. Do not equate finite-horizon capacity calibration with asymptotic minimax resolution.\n\n# 4. Mathematical verdict\n\n**Not solved.** Exact terminal completion is available, but neither a uniform sharp lower witness nor an asymptotically improving full-class upper certificate has been obtained.\n\nThe fixed goal is unchanged: for predetermined nonnegative GD steps, with\nW_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_*)/(L||x_0-x_*||^2), decide whether W_n^*/U_n^ZJ tends to one. The affirmative statement requires every epsilon>0 to admit N_epsilon independent of h such that W_n(h)>=(1-epsilon)U_n^ZJ for every n>=N_epsilon and every h in [0,infinity)^n. A negative resolution requires a fixed delta>0 and certified schedules on unbounded horizons with W_n(h)<=(1-delta)U_n^ZJ.\n\nFor the exact comparator, P_0=B_0=1,\nF(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2,\nG(x,d)=2x+d/2+sqrt(d^2+8xd)/2,\nP_n=max_(k+l=n-1)F(P_k,P_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(P_k,B_l), and U_n^ZJ=1/B_n. No decimal constant or assumed normalized limit replaces this sequence.\n\nClosed components are the six scoped facts listed below. The viable architectures are a full legal correlated adversary with quantitative normalization, or a genuinely stronger upper-certificate composition. The main blocker is a target-scale consequence of the exact representations: a sign-preserving primitive reduction has no automatic objective-scale guarantee. The highest-priority unresolved bridge in the constructive upper route is an actual useful enlargement by signed terminal credit, or a theorem excluding that enlargement. Current evidence is **method failure**, not proposition failure: several entire construction classes fail on specified schedules, but unrestricted W remains undetermined.\n\nCalibration caveat: PARTIAL and SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved; the quantitative interfaces remain unmatched.\n\nThe direct negative route is now a FULL last-iterate objective certificate on the concrete near-critical family, not another intrinsic primitive/terminal-credit condition. Every existing lower-witness exclusion is compatible with the actual schedule having either large or small unrestricted W. An explicit q_m bound with limsup q_m B_(2^m-1)<1 would settle the original problem negatively, even though it only constructs one schedule sequence. Requiring that sequence itself to be primitive would add an unsupported restriction. Fixed-constant neighboring deformations and a prescribed final-step change remain possible, with full physical costs retained.\n\nThe mixed-frequency drafts now contain actual joint interpolants and an all-final-step positive payoff, but their stated support classes still miss the benchmark. The fast/slow model910ba7bac773e2a3 and three-type model041069a3df853d6c control the fast terminal moment by common chronological cycles. Their legal examples do not show that the needed constant is achievable. The critical-scalar low-curvature and selected steep-ramp results likewise remain scoped exclusions; permanent capture after arbitrary core entry is false. General incidence-flow exclusions require their stated history-correlation or profile assumptions.\n\nGeneral credit contractionabc85cd67257bcf4 changes the physical steps and has exact capacity2C/[2+theta(C-1)]; it is not same-schedule repair. Proportional-array uniquenessaad4e2d16318961c and entrywise-dominance obstructionc6a9d1e4910e3681 identify real limitations of such transformations. The complete C_spine repair candidate2d75b4696641736c applies to finite signed certificates on schedules with recursively saturated right-tail cuts ending in at most two positive steps; it forces ordinary earlier-prefix certificates and may change the full PSD remainder. Arbitrary schedules need not have those cuts, and universal validity is not identified with certifiability by this result.\n\nThe near-critical free-terminal relaxation is now addressed by draft70e9da73936cb7b3: two queried-constant cells give K/(1-d0^2 K), preserving the same asymptotic gap from1/B_n. Its graphs encode dual multipliers, unlike the primal coordinate-flow graphs in the relay exclusions.
